      {
        "id": "text-f1-72",
        "x": 140,
        "y": 1400,
        "content": "Every classroom has a smart board drop and a teacher drop, and every AP is a home run to the IDF. Blue rings are Wi Fi coverage at 5 GHz, orange cones are camera views.\nDesks in each room share a group, so a whole room layout moves together.",
        "fontSize": 17,
        "color": "#94a3b8",
        "fontWeight": "normal",
        "fontStyle": "normal",
        "textAlign": "start",
        "textDecoration": "none",
        "bgColor": "#000000",
        "bgEnabled": false,
        "opacity": 1
      }

            "id": "text-f1-72",
            "x": 140,
            "y": 1400,
            "content": "Every classroom has a smart board drop and a teacher drop, and every AP is a home run to the IDF. Blue rings are Wi Fi coverage at 5 GHz, orange cones are camera views.\nDesks in each room share a group, so a whole room layout moves together.",

            "color": "#94a3b8",
